+  },
+  {
+    rules: {
+      "@typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any": "warn"
+    }
+  }
+];
+
+export default eslintConfig;

 import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
 import { ChatWindow } from "@/components/chat/ChatWindow";
+import { useAuth } from "@/context/auth-context";
+import { useConversations, useConversationParticipants } from "@/hooks/use-conversations";
 
 export default function ChatPage() {
   const { chatId } = useParams<{ chatId: string }>();
+  const { user } = useAuth();
+  const { conversations } = useConversations();
+  const conversation = conversations.find((c) => c.id === chatId);
+  const participants = useConversationParticipants(conversation ? [conversation] : [], user?.uid);
+  const otherId = conversation?.participantIds.find((id) => id !== user?.uid) ?? "";
+  const heading = participants[otherId]?.fullName || conversation?.listingTitle || "Conversation";
 
   return (
     <main className="mx-auto flex min-h-[70vh] max-w-4xl flex-col px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
-      <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold">Conversation</h1>
+      <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold">{heading}</h1>
+      {conversation?.listingTitle && participants[otherId]?.fullName ? (
+        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-foreground/60">About: {conversation.listingTitle}</p>
+      ) : null}
       <section className="mt-6 flex min-h-[50vh] flex-1 flex-col rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-soft">
         <ChatWindow conversationId={chatId} />
       </section>

diff --git a/src/components/shared/university-combobox.tsx b/src/components/shared/university-combobox.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..647b882
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/components/shared/university-combobox.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+"use client";
+
+import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
+
+import { UNIVERSITIES } from "@/lib/constants/universities";
+
+export function UniversityCombobox({
+  value,
+  onChange,
+  placeholder = "Type to search universities...",
+  id
+}: {
+  value: string;
+  onChange: (value: string) => void;
+  placeholder?: string;
+  id?: string;
+}) {
+  const [query, setQuery] = useState(value);
+  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
+  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    setQuery(value);
+  }, [value]);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    function handleClickOutside(event: MouseEvent) {
+      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
+        setOpen(false);
+        setQuery(value);
+      }
+    }
+
+    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
+    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
+  }, [value]);
+
+  const matches = UNIVERSITIES.filter((university) =>
+    university.toLowerCase().includes(query.trim().toLowerCase())
+  ).slice(0, 8);
+
+  return (
+    <div ref={containerRef} className="relative">
+      <input
+        id={id}
+        type="text"
+        value={query}
+        placeholder={placeholder}
+        onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
+        onChange={(event) => {
+          setQuery(event.target.value);
+          setOpen(true);
+          if (!event.target.value) onChange("");
+        }}
+        className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#eadfce] bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm"
+        autoComplete="off"
+      />
+      {open && matches.length > 0 ? (
+        <ul className="absolute z-20 mt-1 max-h-64 w-full overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-[#eadfce] bg-white shadow-lg">
+          {matches.map((university) => (
+            <li key={university}>
+              <button
+                type="button"
+                onClick={() => {
+                  onChange(university);
+                  setQuery(university);
+                  setOpen(false);
+                }}
+                className="block w-full px-4 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-[#faf6ef]"
+              >
+                {university}
+              </button>
+            </li>
+          ))}
+        </ul>
+      ) : null}
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+export default UniversityCombobox;
diff --git a/src/hooks/use-conversations.ts b/src/hooks/use-conversations.ts
index 504d4c9..d852495 100644
--- a/src/hooks/use-conversations.ts
+++ b/src/hooks/use-conversations.ts
@@ -4,9 +4,56 @@ import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
 
 import { useAuth } from "@/context/auth-context";
 import { ensureConversation, getConversationsForUser } from "@/lib/data/conversations";
+import { getProfileById } from "@/lib/data/profiles";
 import { debugSupabaseError, friendlySupabaseError } from "@/lib/utils/errors";
 import { type AsyncStatus, withRetry } from "@/lib/utils/retry";
 import type { Conversation } from "@/types/chat";
+import type { UserProfile } from "@/types/user";
+
+export function getOtherParticipantId(conversation: Pick<Conversation, "participantIds">, currentUserId?: string) {
+  return conversation.participantIds.find((participantId) => participantId !== currentUserId);
+}
+
+// Resolves the *other* participant's profile for each conversation, so chat
+// lists/headers can show "Yeraly Zharylkassyn" instead of a generic
+// "Conversation" placeholder or the listing title.
+export function useConversationParticipants(conversations: Conversation[], currentUserId?: string) {
+  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState<Record<string, UserProfile | null>>({});
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    let cancelled = false;
+
+    async function load() {
+      if (!currentUserId || !conversations.length) {
+        if (!cancelled) {
+          setProfiles(prev => Object.keys(prev).length === 0 ? prev : {});
+        }
+        return;
+      }
+
+      const otherIds = [
+        ...new Set(
+          conversations
+            .map((conversation) => getOtherParticipantId(conversation, currentUserId))
+            .filter((id): id is string => Boolean(id))
+        )
+      ];
+
+      const entries = await Promise.all(
+        otherIds.map(async (id) => [id, await getProfileById(id).catch(() => null)] as const)
+      );
+
+      if (!cancelled) setProfiles(Object.fromEntries(entries));
+    }
+
+    void load();
+    return () => {
+      cancelled = true;
+    };
+  }, [conversations, currentUserId]);
+
+  return profiles;
+}
 
 export function useConversations() {
   const { user } = useAuth();

+  ["kostanay", "Kostanay"],
+  ["atyrau", "Atyrau"],
+  ["utemisov", "Oral"],
+  ["west kazakhstan", "Oral"],
+  ["kozybayev", "Petropavlovsk"],
+  ["north kazakhstan", "Petropavlovsk"]
+];
+
+export function getUniversityCity(university: string | undefined | null): string {
+  const value = (university ?? "").toLowerCase();
+  const match = UNIVERSITY_CITY_ENTRIES.find(([needle]) => value.includes(needle));
+  return match?.[1] ?? "Almaty";
+}

new file mode 100644

@@ -0,0 +1,42 @@
+-- Run this once in the Supabase SQL editor (Dashboard -> SQL Editor -> New query).
+-- Safe to re-run: every statement is guarded with IF NOT EXISTS.
+
+-- 1. University field on listings (this is what "list a room" and the
+--    university-filter feature actually needed to work correctly).
+alter table public.listings add column if not exists university text;
+
+-- 2. Coordinates for a future map view (Google Maps JS API or a free
+--    alternative like Leaflet/OpenStreetMap - see chat notes). Nullable so
+--    existing listings keep working without them.
+alter table public.listings add column if not exists lat double precision;
+alter table public.listings add column if not exists lng double precision;
+
+-- 3. Saved searches, so a user's filters can be stored and revisited/alerted on.
+create table if not exists public.saved_searches (
+  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
+  user_id uuid not null references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
+  name text not null,
+  filters jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,
+  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
+);
+
+alter table public.saved_searches enable row level security;
+
+drop policy if exists select_saved_searches_owner on public.saved_searches;
+drop policy if exists insert_saved_searches_owner on public.saved_searches;
+drop policy if exists delete_saved_searches_owner on public.saved_searches;
+
+create policy select_saved_searches_owner
+  on public.saved_searches
+  for select
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
+
+create policy insert_saved_searches_owner
+  on public.saved_searches
+  for insert
+  with check (auth.uid() = user_id);
+
+create policy delete_saved_searches_owner
+  on public.saved_searches
+  for delete
+  using (auth.uid() = user_id);
